UI_Lesson_Glassmaking_Intermediate_P2::en = "Casting is the most accessible technique. Pour molten glass into a mold and let it cool. Sand molds work for rough shapes — pack damp sand around a form, remove the form, and pour glass into the cavity. For smoother results, use a clay or plaster mold that has been thoroughly dried to prevent steam explosions.",
UI_Lesson_Glassmaking_Intermediate_P2::zh-hans::processed::unverified = "铸造是最易上手的技术。将熔融玻璃倒入模具并让其冷却。砂模适用于粗糙形状——将湿砂围绕模型压实，移除模型，然后将玻璃倒入空腔。要获得更光滑的结果，使用已彻底干燥以防止蒸汽爆炸的粘土或石膏模具。",
UI_Lesson_Glassmaking_Intermediate_P3::en = "Slumping softens existing glass over a mold. Place a flat piece of glass over a bowl-shaped mold in a kiln. As the kiln heats up, the glass softens and sags into the mold shape. This technique repurposes flat glass (window panes, mirror glass) into curved bowls, dishes, and containers without reaching full melting point.",
UI_Lesson_Glassmaking_Intermediate_P3::zh-hans::processed::unverified = "热弯是通过模具软化现有玻璃。将一块平板玻璃放在窑炉中的碗状模具上。随着窑炉升温，玻璃软化并垂落成模具形状。该技术将平板玻璃（窗玻璃、镜面玻璃）重塑为弯曲的碗、盘和容器，无需达到完全熔点。",
UI_Lesson_Glassmaking_Intermediate_P4::en = "Annealing is essential for any formed glass object. Glass that cools too quickly develops internal stress that causes spontaneous shattering — sometimes hours or days later.
